\nfrom {PID}.VT_A_PLOA_CREDIT_CARD_PROV_OUTER_FINANG_DTL_TAB_03 t1
\nwhere PROM_PROJECTS    like 'QCFQ%'  
\nand     LICEN_PLATE_NO not like '冀%' 
\nand CUST_TYPE ='车分期' 
\nand ACCT_STS_DESC <>'核销'
\n
\n  
\n)a
\n
\nleft join   (select IDEN_NO,ACTIVA_DT,PROM_ORG_NO
\n,row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY IDEN_NO ORDER BY ACTIVA_DT asc) AS rn
\nfrom {PID}.A_D_LN_CREDIT_CARD_INF
\nwhere DATA_DT=DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}'
\n
\n ) a1
\non a.IDEN_NO= a1.IDEN_NO 
\nand a1.rn=1
\n
\nleft join (select "IDEN_NO",sum(loan_bal) as  loan_bal
\nfrom  {PID}.VT_A_PLOA_CREDIT_CARD_PROV_OUTER_FINANG_DTL_TAB_03 t1
\nwhere ACCT_STS not in ('W','WQ')
\ngroup by 1)b
\non A.IDEN_NO=b.IDEN_NO
\n
\nleft join (
\nselect A.custr_nbr  
\n
\n,sum(CASE WHEN B.acct_credit_limit-A.LOAN_BAL< 0 then 0 else  B.acct_credit_limit-A.LOAN_BAL   end)   as  credit_limit
\nfrom (select  
\nt1.custr_nbr
\n,sum(case when CAST((t1.BAL_INTFLAG||t1.bal_int) AS decimal(32,2))  +  CAST((t1.STMBALINTFLAG||t1.stm_balint)AS decimal(32,2)) >=0 
\n             then  t1.NOT_OUT_ACCT_RCPT_CONSM_BAL
\n            + t1.CHECK_CONSM_BAL
\n            + CAST((t1.BAL_INTFLAG||t1.bal_int) AS decimal(32,2))
\n            + CAST((t1.STMBALINTFLAG||t1.stm_balint) AS decimal(32,2))
\n            + cast((t1.STAGES_PAY_NOT_OUT_ACCT_RCPT_BAL_SYMBO||t1.STAGES_PAY_NOT_OUT_ACCT_RCPT_BAL) AS decimal(32,2))
\n            + cast((t1.STAGES_PAY_POSTED_RCPT_BAL_SYMBO||t1.STAGES_PAY_POSTED_RCPT_BAL) AS decimal(32,2)) 
\n           
\n            + nvl(t2.PRESS_DAY_LOAN_P_AMTZ_BAL,0)
\n            + nvl(t2.CNY_STAGES_P_AMTZ_BAL,0) 
\n         
\n         else (nvl(t2.PRESS_DAY_LOAN_P_AMTZ_BAL,0) + nvl(t2.CNY_STAGES_P_AMTZ_BAL,0) ) end )    AS LOAN_BAL         
\nfrom  fdm.F_AC_CCRD_CNY_CREDIT_ACCT t1    
\nleft join fdm.F_AC_CCRD_ADDIT_SMS_SERVI_ACCT t2
\non t1.ACCT_NO=t2.ACCT_NO
\nAND t2.end_dt > DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}'-1
\nAND t2.start_dt <= DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}'-1 
\nWHERE   t1.end_dt > DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}' -1
\nAND t1.start_dt <= DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}' -1  and t1.ACCT_STS_CD  NOT in('WQ','W')   
\n 
\ngroup by 1)A
\nleft join (SELECT
\n    TMP.custr_nbr,
\n    MAX(all_cards.acct_credit_limit)AS acct_credit_limit  
\nFROM (
\n    
\n    SELECT
\n        t1.custr_nbr,
\n        NVL(t1.acct_credit_limit, 0) AS acct_credit_limit  
\n    FROM fdm.F_AC_CCRD_CNY_CREDIT_ACCT t1
\n    
\n    LEFT JOIN (
\n        SELECT DISTINCT acct_no, CARD_LOGOUT_CD
\n        FROM fdm.F_MD_CCRD_CARD_INFO
\n        WHERE end_dt > DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}' - 1
\n          AND start_dt <= DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}' - 1
\n    ) t3 ON t1.acct_no = t3.acct_no
\n    
\n    LEFT JOIN fdm.F_AC_CCRD_ADDIT_SMS_SERVI_ACCT t2
\n        ON t1.ACCT_NO = t2.ACCT_NO
\n        AND t2.end_dt > DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}' - 1
\n        AND t2.start_dt <= DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}' - 1
\n    WHERE
\n        t1.end_dt > DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}' - 1
\n        AND t1.start_dt <= DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}' - 1
\n        AND t1.ACCT_STS_CD =''
\n        AND t3.CARD_LOGOUT_CD = ''  
\n     
\n) tmp
\n
\nJOIN (
\n    SELECT
\n        custr_nbr,
\n        NVL(acct_credit_limit, 0) AS acct_credit_limit
\n    FROM fdm.F_AC_CCRD_CNY_CREDIT_ACCT
\n    WHERE end_dt > DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}' - 1
\n      AND start_dt <= DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}' - 1
\n    
\n\tand ACCT_STS_CD    NOT IN ('WQ', 'W')
\n) all_cards ON tmp.custr_nbr = all_cards.custr_nbr
\nGROUP BY custr_nbr
\n)B
\non A.custr_nbr= B.custr_nbr
\ngroup by 1)c
\nON A.IDEN_NO=c.custr_nbr
\n
\nleft join (select 
\nIDEN_NO
\n,max(LOAN_BAL_REMAI_TERM) as LOAN_BAL_REMAI_TERM
\nfrom {PID}.VT_A_PLOA_CREDIT_CARD_PROV_OUTER_FINANG_DTL_TAB_03
\nwhere ACCT_STS_DESC='正常' and CARD_STS='正常' and PROM_PROJECTS not like 'QCFQ%' 
\n
\ngroup by 1)d
\nON A.IDEN_NO=d.IDEN_NO 
\nleft join cdm.c_org_org_inf  org
\non org.org_no =a1.PROM_ORG_NO
\nand org.data_dt =DATE'{ETL_TX_DT}'
\nwhere a.province<>'剔除'

\n          ,WHE_JSD                  
\n          ,WHE_CDD                  
\n          ,DATA_SOURC                
\n      )
\n      select
\n         DATA_DT                  
\n        ,ORG_NO                   
\n        ,BELO_LINE                
\n        ,CURRCY_CD                
\n        ,PROD_NO                  
\n        ,RATE_RMB                 
\n        ,RECVBL_INTST             
\n        ,ACCRU_INTST              
\n        ,WHE_VLD                  
\n        ,WHE_JSD                  
\n        ,WHE_CDD                  
\n        ,DATA_SOURC                
\n      from
\n      (
\n          SELECT 
\n          DATE '{ETL_TX_DT}'     as DATA_DT
\n          ,ORG_NO                as ORG_NO               
\n          ,LINE_TYPE             as BELO_LINE              
\n          ,'156'                 as CURRCY_CD        
\n          ,BIZ_VATY              AS PROD_NO        
\n          ,1                     AS RATE_RMB          
\n          ,nvl(RECVBL_INTST,0)   as RECVBL_INTST     
\n          ,nvl(ACCRU_INTST,0)    as ACCRU_INTST   
\n          ,'Y'                   as WHE_VLD
\n          ,'N'                   as WHE_JSD
\n          ,'N'                   as WHE_CDD
\n          ,CASE WHEN  T.DATA_SOURC = ('JSD') then 'XD'
\n                when  t.DATA_SOURC = ('HLW') THEN 'HLW'
\n                WHEN T.DATA_SOURC IN ('XYK','QCJR') THEN 'XYK'
\n          else t.data_sourc 
\n          end                    as DATA_SOURC            
\n          FROM PID.A_G_LN_RECVBL_ACCRU_INTST_SUM T 
\n          WHERE T.DATA_DT = DATE '{ETL_TX_DT}'
\n          AND CURRCY_CD = '001'
\n          and DATA_SOURC not in ('XD','RYD') 
\n          AND (NVL(T.RECVBL_INTST,0) <> 0 OR NVL(T.ACCRU_INTST,0) <> 0)
\n          
\n          union all
\n          
\n          
\n          select
\n          DATE '{ETL_TX_DT}'   AS  DATA_DT          
\n          ,t.ORG_NO            AS  ORG_NO           
\n          ,case when (BIZ_CD = '1539' and ACCT_NO_SERIAL_NO = '00001')  then '02'  else '01' end                AS  LINE_TYPE        
\n          ,'156'               AS  CURRCY_CD        
\n          ,NULL
\n          ,1
\n          ,0                   AS  RECVBL_INTST
\n          ,nvl(sum(nvl((DEBTOR_BAL_RMB - CREDITOR_BAL_RMB),0)),0)  AS ACCRU_INTST    
\n          ,'Y'
\n          ,'N'
\n          ,'N'
\n          ,'XD'  
\n          from pid.a_d_fi_intal_acct t 
\n          where t.DATA_DT = '{ETL_TX_DT}' 
\n          and subj_no like '1501%'\t\t  
\n          and ((BIZ_CD = '1539' and ACCT_NO_SERIAL_NO = '00001')  
\n          or (t.ACCT_CN_NAME like '应计小额线上%' and substr(t.ACCT_NO,5,13) IN ('9010213900013','9010214200012')))  
\n          group by t.ORG_NO,BIZ_CD,ACCT_NO_SERIAL_NO
\n          having nvl(sum(nvl((DEBTOR_BAL_RMB - CREDITOR_BAL_RMB),0)),0) <> 0
\n\t\t  
\n\t\t  
\n\t\t   union all
\n          
\n          
\n          select
\n          DATE '{ETL_TX_DT}'   AS  DATA_DT          
\n          ,t.ORG_NO            AS  ORG_NO           
\n          ,'01'                AS  LINE_TYPE        
\n          ,'156'               AS  CURRCY_CD        
\n          ,NULL
\n          ,1
\n          ,nvl(sum(nvl((DEBTOR_BAL_RMB - CREDITOR_BAL_RMB),0)),0)  AS RECVBL_INTST    
\n\t\t  ,0                                                       as ACCRU_INTST
\n          ,'Y'
\n          ,'N'
\n          ,'N'
\n          ,'XD'  
\n          from pid.a_d_fi_intal_acct t 
\n          where t.DATA_DT = '{ETL_TX_DT}' 
\n          and subj_no like '1502%'\t\t  
\n          and t.ACCT_CN_NAME like '应收小额线上%' 
\n\t\t  and substr(t.ACCT_NO,5,13) IN ('9010214000013','9010214300012')  
\n          group by t.ORG_NO
\n          having nvl(sum(nvl((DEBTOR_BAL_RMB - CREDITOR_BAL_RMB),0)),0) <> 0
\n      ) t

RR1.BAL_CHAR_CD='00' THEN ROUND(debtor_half_year_calcu_base*RT2.RATE_RMB,2)\n    WHEN RR1.BAL_CHAR_CD='01' THEN ROUND(creditor_half_year_calcu_base*RT2.RATE_RMB,2)\n    ELSE\n     CASE WHEN RR1.GL_TYPE='1' THEN ROUND(debtor_half_year_calcu_base*RT2.RATE_RMB,2)-ROUND(creditor_half_year_calcu_base*RT2.RATE_RMB,2)\n       WHEN RR1.GL_TYPE='2' THEN ROUND(creditor_half_year_calcu_base*RT2.RATE_RMB,2)-ROUND(debtor_half_year_calcu_base*RT2.RATE_RMB,2)\n    END\n    END\n  WHEN RR1.ADD_MNS_IND='1' THEN\n    CASE WHEN RR1.BAL_CHAR_CD='00' THEN ROUND(debtor_half_year_calcu_base*(-1)*RT2.RATE_RMB,2)\n    WHEN RR1.BAL_CHAR_CD='01' THEN ROUND(creditor_half_year_calcu_base*(-1)*RT2.RATE_RMB,2)\n    ELSE\n     (CASE WHEN RR1.GL_TYPE='1' THEN  ELSE 0\n                   END)                AS  CARD_LVL_3          \n             ,SUM(CASE WHEN T2.HIGH_CARD_TYPE IN ('钻石卡','IC如意财富卡')\n                   THEN 1  ELSE 0\n                   END)                AS  CARD_LVL_4          \n          FROM ODM.O_CRBS_VYKTD T1 \n             INNER JOIN PID.VT_A_G_AG_DEBIT_CARD_ISSUE_SITU_MONTH_01 T2  \n\t\t\t    ON T1.KAHAOO = T2.KAHAOO\n DATE '{ETL_TX_DT}' BETWEEN T1.START_DT AND T1.END_DT -1\n             AND T1.HNKACS=0   \n\t\t\t AND LENGTH(T1.KAHAOO) = 19\n             AND SUBSTR(T1.KAHAOO,1,6) IN ('623582','622498','623000','622499','603413','940046','623265')\t\n\t\t\t AND NOT (SUBSTR(T1.KAHAOO,1,8) IN ('62358230','62358235') AND T1.KAAAJZ='9')  \n             AND (T1.KAAAJZ = '1' or T1.KAAAJZ = '2' )      \n             AND T1.KAAAXZ='0'\t\t\t\t \n          GROUP BY\n\t\t\t T3.ORG_NO,\n\t\t\t T3.ORG_ABRE,\n\t\t\t T3.LEVEL_SCD_ORG_NO,\n\t\t\t T3.level_scd_org_abre\n\t\t\t \n\t\t\t \n      UNION ALL\n          SELECT \n             SUBSTR(DATE '{ETL_TX_DT}',1,7)         AS   STAT_MTH            \n             ,'2GR0004'                  AS   REPORT_ID           \n             ,T3.ORG_NO                                           \n             ,'IC借记卡新发卡量'           AS   TEXT1             \n             ,T3.ORG_ABRE                                         \n             ,T3.LEVEL_SCD_ORG_NO                                 \n\t\t\t ,T3.level_scd_org_abre                               \n\t         ,(CASE WHEN T3.ORG_NO   LIKE '04%' THEN '天津' \n                    WHEN T3.ORG_NO   LIKE '08%' THEN '山东' \n                    ELSE '河北' end  )  AS ORG_PROV               \n             ,SUM(CASE WHEN T2.HIGH_CARD_TYPE NOT IN ('金卡','白金卡','钻石卡','IC如意金卡','IC如意财富卡')\n                   THEN 1  ELSE 0\n                   END)               AS  CARD_LVL_1              \n             ,SUM(CASE WHEN T2.HIGH_CARD_TYPE IN ('金卡','IC如意金卡')\n                   THEN 1  ELSE 0        \n                   END)                AS  CARD_LVL_2             \n             ,SUM(CASE WHEN T2.HIGH_CARD_TYPE IN ('白金卡')\n                   THEN 1  ELSE 0\n                   END)                AS  CARD_LVL_3             \n             ,SUM(CASE WHEN T2.HIGH_CARD_TYPE IN ('钻石卡','IC如意财富卡')\n                   THEN 1  ELSE 0\n                   END)                AS  CARD_LVL_4
